## 先看結論

如果你已經有正在考慮的幼兒園，這篇最重要的功能不是告訴你「私幼平均多少」，而是讓你**直接找到那一間幼兒園，看目前查得到的完整費用**。

私立幼兒園可能同時包含學費、雜費、材料費、活動費、午餐費、點心費、交通費、課後延托費與家長會費。

> 115學年度＝2026年8月～2027年7月；114學年度＝2025年8月～2026年7月。若115完整費用尚未公開，本文保留114學年度資料作為預算參考，但不把它寫成115最新現價。

<div class="kindergarten-search" role="search">
<label for="kindergarten-search-input">🔍 搜尋幼兒園名稱</label>
<div class="kindergarten-search-box"><input id="kindergarten-search-input" type="search" autocomplete="off" placeholder="例如：康橋、奇異鳥、衛理"><button id="kindergarten-search-clear" type="button">清除</button></div>
<p id="kindergarten-search-count" class="kindergarten-search-count">輸入至少2個字開始搜尋</p>
<div id="kindergarten-search-results" class="kindergarten-search-results"></div>
</div>

<script is:inline>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const normalize = (value) => (value || "").toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "").replace(/台/g, "臺");
  const input = document.getElementById("kindergarten-search-input");
  const clearButton = document.getElementById("kindergarten-search-clear");
  const resultBox = document.getElementById("kindergarten-search-results");
  const count = document.getElementById("kindergarten-search-count");
  if (!input || !clearButton || !resultBox || !count) return;

  const cards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("details.kindergarten-fee-card")).map((card) => {
    const name = card.querySelector("summary strong")?.textContent?.trim() || "";
    const location = card.dataset.location || "";
    return { card, id: card.id, name, location, search: normalize(`${name} ${location}`) };
  });

  const render = () => {
    const query = normalize(input.value);
    resultBox.innerHTML = "";
    if (query.length < 2) {
      count.textContent = "輸入至少 2 個字開始搜尋";
      return;
    }
    const allMatches = cards.filter((item) => item.search.includes(query));
    const matches = allMatches.slice(0, 50);
    if (!allMatches.length) {
      count.textContent = "找不到相符園所，試試園名中的其他關鍵字";
      return;
    }
    count.textContent = `找到 ${allMatches.length} 間相符園所${allMatches.length > 50 ? "，先顯示前 50 筆" : ""}`;
    matches.forEach((item) => {
      const button = document.createElement("button");
      button.type = "button";
      button.className = "kindergarten-search-result";
      button.innerHTML = `<strong>${item.name}</strong><span>${item.location}</span>`;
      button.addEventListener("click", () => {
        item.card.open = true;
        history.replaceState(null, "", `#${item.id}`);
        item.card.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
        setTimeout(() => item.card.querySelector("summary")?.focus(), 450);
      });
      resultBox.appendChild(button);
    });
  };

  input.addEventListener("input", render);
  clearButton.addEventListener("click", () => {
    input.value = "";
    resultBox.innerHTML = "";
    count.textContent = "輸入至少 2 個字開始搜尋";
    input.focus();
  });
  if (location.hash) {
    const target = document.querySelector(location.hash);
    if (target?.matches?.("details.kindergarten-fee-card")) target.open = true;
  }
});
</script>

<script is:inline>
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
  const money = (value) => {
    const raw = String(value ?? "").trim();
    if (!raw) return "—";
    if (/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(raw)) {
      return "NT$" + Number(raw).toLocaleString("zh-TW");
    }
    return raw.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  };

  const clean = (value) =>
    String(value ?? "")
      .replace(/\r/g, " ")
      .replace(/\n/g, " ")
      .replace(/\t/g, " ")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();

  const escapeHtml = (value) =>
    String(value ?? "")
      .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
      .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
      .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
      .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
      .replace(/'/g, "&#039;");

  const feeOrder = [
    "學費","雜費","材料費","活動費","午餐費","點心費",
    "全學期總收費","交通費","課後延托費","家長會費"
  ];

  const renderClass = (age, semester, months, className, fees) => {
    const rows = feeOrder.map((feeName) => {
      const fee = fees?.[feeName] || {};
      const period = clean(fee["收費期間"]);
      const unit = clean(fee["單價"]);
      const subtotal = clean(fee["小計"]);
      const rowClass =
        feeName === "全學期總收費" ? "total-row" : "";
      return `<tr class="${rowClass}">
        <th>${escapeHtml(feeName)}</th>
        <td>${escapeHtml(period || "—")}</td>
        <td>${escapeHtml(money(unit))}</td>
        <td>${escapeHtml(money(subtotal))}</td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join("");

    return `<section class="kindergarten-fee-combination">
      <h5>${escapeHtml(age)}歲・${escapeHtml(semester)}・${escapeHtml(className)}</h5>
      <p class="fee-note">本學期收費月數：${escapeHtml(months || "—")}個月</p>
      <div class="kindergarten-fee-grid">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>費用項目</th>
              <th>收費期間</th>
              <th>單價</th>
              <th>小計</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            ${rows}
            <tr class="actual-row">
              <th>補助後家長實付</th>
              <td colspan="3">依園所類型、幼兒年齡、胎次與家庭補助資格計算；請以當年度核定資格為準</td>
            </tr>
            <tr>
              <th>其他費用</th>
              <td colspan="3">制服、書包、保險、校外教學、寒暑假或其他園方另行公告項目，若上表未列則需另向園方確認</td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </section>`;
  };

  const renderSlip = (data) => {
    const slip = data?.slip || {};
    const combinations = [];

    Object.entries(slip).forEach(([age, ageData]) => {
      Object.entries(ageData || {}).forEach(([semester, semesterData]) => {
        const months = semesterData?.months || "";
        Object.entries(semesterData?.class || {}).forEach(([className, fees]) => {
          const hasAnyValue = feeOrder.some((feeName) => {
            const fee = fees?.[feeName] || {};
            return clean(fee["單價"]) || clean(fee["小計"]);
          });
          if (hasAnyValue) {
            combinations.push({ age, semester, months, className, fees });
          }
        });
      });
    });

    if (!combinations.length) {
      return `<p class="kindergarten-fee-error">目前沒有可顯示的逐項收費明細。</p>`;
    }

    const tabs = combinations.map((item, index) =>
      `<button type="button" class="kindergarten-fee-tab" data-fee-tab="${index}" aria-selected="${index === 0 ? "true" : "false"}">
        ${escapeHtml(item.age)}歲・${escapeHtml(item.semester)}・${escapeHtml(item.className)}
      </button>`
    ).join("");

    const panels = combinations.map((item, index) =>
      `<div data-fee-panel="${index}" ${index === 0 ? "" : "hidden"}>
        ${renderClass(item.age, item.semester, item.months, item.className, item.fees)}
      </div>`
    ).join("");

    return `<h4>完整費用明細</h4>
      <div class="kindergarten-fee-tabs" role="tablist">${tabs}</div>
      <div>${panels}</div>`;
  };

  const bindTabs = (container) => {
    container.querySelectorAll("[data-fee-tab]").forEach((button) => {
      button.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const index = button.dataset.feeTab;
        container.querySelectorAll("[data-fee-tab]").forEach((b) =>
          b.setAttribute("aria-selected", String(b === button))
        );
        container.querySelectorAll("[data-fee-panel]").forEach((panel) => {
          panel.hidden = panel.dataset.feePanel !== index;
        });
      });
    });
  };

  const loadCard = async (card) => {
    if (card.dataset.feeLoaded === "true") return;
    const url = card.dataset.feeJson;
    const container = card.querySelector("[data-full-fee]");
    if (!url || !container) return;

    card.dataset.feeLoaded = "loading";

    try {
      const response = await fetch(url, { cache: "force-cache" });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error("fee fetch failed");
      const data = await response.json();
      container.innerHTML = renderSlip(data);
      bindTabs(container);
      card.dataset.feeLoaded = "true";
    } catch (error) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<p class="kindergarten-fee-error">完整逐項費用暫時無法載入，請稍後再試。</p>';
      card.dataset.feeLoaded = "false";
    }
  };

  document.querySelectorAll("details.kindergarten-fee-card[data-fee-json]").forEach((card) => {
    card.addEventListener("toggle", () => {
      if (card.open) loadCard(card);
    });

    if (card.open) loadCard(card);
  });
});
</script>


## 正在幫3～5歲孩子選幼兒園？

查完學費後，很多家長下一個問題是：**要不要為了英文環境選雙語／全美語幼兒園？如果不讀全美幼，英文可以怎麼安排？**

如果你也正在做這個選擇，可以接著看：

- [全美語幼兒園值得嗎？我們為什麼重排每月2萬元教育預算](/blog/not-all-english-kindergarten/)
- [4歲零基礎學英文怎麼開始？我們比較全美幼、雙語幼、補習班、真人外師後的選擇](/blog/how-to-choose-english-for-preschoolers/)
- [幼兒英文啟蒙怎麼開始？3～6歲家庭實作路線圖](/blog/preschool-english-beginner-guide/)

## 115學年度公立、非營利、準公共費用

| 類型 | 第1胎 | 第2胎 | 第3胎以上 |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| 公立幼兒園 | 每月最高NT$1,000 | 免繳 | 免繳 |
| 非營利幼兒園 | 每月最高NT$2,000 | 每月最高NT$1,000 | 免繳 |
| 準公共幼兒園 | 每月最高NT$3,000 | 每月最高NT$2,000 | 每月最高NT$1,000 |
| 一般私立幼兒園 | 依園所收費 | 依園所收費 | 依園所收費 |

交通、保險、延托等仍可能另外計算。

### 如果不讀雙語／全美語幼兒園，英文怎麼辦？

選公立、非營利、準公共或一般私幼，不代表孩子就沒有英文環境。我們家替4歲女兒找英文學習方式時，也比較過全美幼、雙語幼、補習班、英文 App 和真人外師，後來把「幼兒園怎麼選」和「英文怎麼學」拆成兩個問題。

→ [4歲零基礎學英文怎麼開始？我們比較全美幼、雙語幼、補習班、真人外師後的完整歷程](/blog/how-to-choose-english-for-preschoolers/)


## 私立幼兒園費用怎麼看？

每個行政區都依「**完整費用 → 有全學期／延托資料 → 只有費用摘要 → 尚未查到費用**」排序，所以家長會先看到資訊最完整的園所。

表格中的「—」代表目前取得的公開資料沒有列出該項，不代表一定免費。


### 一年多花十幾萬讀全美語幼兒園，值得嗎？

真正值得比較的，不只是「一般幼兒園多少錢」和「全美語幼兒園多少錢」，而是：**如果不把全部英文預算放在幼兒園，省下來的預算能不能組合出另一種更適合孩子的英文環境？**

→ [全美語幼兒園值得嗎？我們為什麼重排每月2萬元教育預算](/blog/not-all-english-kindergarten/)
